Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in martingales theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 220 and the invariant I(A,B) = 101 mod 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to martingales theory, we decompose the problem into 220 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 101. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)
